## Document 001: 10 月の金融政策決定会合は利上げ見送りへ 政策金利の２％超えは？

Source PDF: all report pdf/10 月の金融政策決定会合は利上げ見送りへ 政策金利の２％超えは？.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 14 日(水)

1/3 
10 月の金融政策決定会合は利上げ見送りへ  
政策金利の２％超えは？ 
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・10月30日の金融政策決定会合に先立って、早くも観測記事が金融市場を賑わせている。米系
通信は「日銀は今月会合で利上げ見送る公算大、12月の可能性排除せず」と報じた。記事内
では「経済・物価は日銀の見通しに沿って推移しており、２会合連続での利上げが必要なほ
ど物価の上振れリスクが一段と強まっている情勢にはない」、とはいえ「一時的な要因を除
いた基調的な物価上昇率は上昇を続けており、目標の２％から上振れていくリスクには依然
として注意が必要だ」などとする日銀関係者の発言が記述されていた。また既報のとおり、
基調的物価上昇率が概ね２％に達したとの判断を展望レポートに記すことにも触れられてい
た。

・10月の利上げ見送りの根拠として、昨日発表された９月の企業物価指数は一定の存在感があ
る。国内企業物価指数は前月比▲0.3％、前年比＋6.8％と上昇が一服し、市場予想の同＋
7.6％も下回った。原油価格が高止まりするなか、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大など
を背景に非鉄金属や化学製品などで上昇が続いた一方、米を中心とする農林水産物の価格下
落が下押しに寄与した。円ベースの輸入物価指数は前年比＋24.5％と大幅な上昇を記録した
・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/3 
が、８月の同＋25.1％からは小幅に鈍化した。川上段階のインフレ圧力は依然として強いも
のの、９月会合時点の判断に大きな修正を迫るものではなかったと考えられる。

・同日発表された「生活意識に関するアンケート調査」（９月調査）も似たような結果であっ
た。５年後の物価予想については「かなり上がる」の割合が51.1％と前回調査対比で概ね横
ばいとなり、「少し上がる」との合計では86.5％と高止まりした。他方、今後５年間の物価
上昇率（年平均）は、中央値が５％で横ばいも、平均値は＋10.1％と前回調査対比で0.7％pt
低下した。ガソリン代の補助に加え、米価の下落が効いたとみられ、家計の予想物価上昇率
が一段と上放れする事態は回避された。政府による家計支援策が効いているとはいえ、家計
の予想物価上昇率が低下したことは、日銀に時間的余裕を与え得る。なお、景況感について
は「１年前との比較」と「１年後の予想」の双方とも大きな変化は認められなかった。

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
・先行きの金融政策はどうなるであろうか。筆者は、10月会合は利上げ見送り、12月会合で利
上げを決定し、政策金利は1.5％で越年すると予想する。Ｆｅｄの利上げ観測が燻り、円安圧
力が強いなか、日銀が金融引き締め方向への手を緩めるとなれば、一段と円安が進行しかね
ない。また日銀短観（９月調査）を見る限り、企業の金融環境に大きな変化はみられず、既
往の利上げが実体経済に染み渡っている様子はそれほど窺えない。これらを踏まえ、筆者は
2027年も３～４回の利上げがあり、政策金利は２％を超えることを想定している。

・ただし、Ｆｅｄが2027年に政策金利を据え置き、その次の政策変更が利下げになるとの見方
が生じれば、日銀は円安対策から解放され、利上げを中断ないしは終了する可能性がある。
その点、ウィリアムズ・ＮＹ連銀総裁とウォラー理事の発言は注視しておきたい。まずウィ
リアムズ総裁は10月13日に「来年はインフレがかなり鈍化し、通年でみれば、全体のインフ
レ率は目標⁠の２％近辺になるとみている。これは私が言及した要因、すなわち関税の影響が
完全に過去のものとなることを反映している」として、2027年に利上げは必要ないとの見方
を示した。

・⁠ウォラー理事は10月13日に「最近の米国債利回りの上昇は経済成長の加速とＡＩへの大規模
投資によるもので、インフレ期待の高まりが原因ではない」、「インフレ期待は引き続き
『しっかりと安定している』」と述べ、その上で「データセンター建設を含むＡＩ投資が経
済の需要を支えており、今後２～３年はその効果が続く⁠可能性がある。大幅な生産性向上が
実現するのは、なお『数年先』だ」とした。同理事の発言を整理すると、①予想インフレは
安定しており、その点において現在の金融政策は適切である、②ＡＩ投資が一巡すれば、そ
れに起因するインフレ圧力は鈍化する、③その後に生産性向上によるディスインフレ方向へ
の動きが予想される、といったところであろう。

・両氏の予想が現実のものになれば、Ｆｅｄの利上げ観測は後退し、場合によっては2027年に
利下げ観測が台頭することもあり得る。そうした環境下、日銀は利上げペースを緩める、或
いは終了を検討するなど、多くの選択肢を手にすることができる。

### Page 1

⻉莱德第三季度净流⼊ 2050 亿美元，营收同⽐增⻓ 19% ⾄ 77.5 亿美元。旗舰 iShares ETF
吸⾦逾 1350 亿美元，私募信贷净流⼊环⽐增加 50 亿美元。 CEO Larry Fink 表⽰正以加速势
头进⼊第四季度，私募市场及退休基⾦业务储备充⾜。但穆迪警告，⾼利率环境下私募信
贷市场⻛险仍在积聚。
摘要：
⻉莱德第三季度资产管理规模突破 15 万亿美元，刷新纪录，强劲的净流⼊与多元业务布局推动
营收同⽐⼤涨 19% ，私募信贷业务亦展现出相对韧性。
10 ⽉ 14 ⽇美股盘前，⻉莱德公布第三季度财报，公司资产管理规模达 15.49 万亿美元，刷新历史
纪录，这⼀惊⼈的数字，已相当于美国全年名义 GDP （约  31 万亿⾄ 32.5 万亿美元）的⼀半。
⻉莱德当季净流⼊规模达 2050 亿美元。营收同⽐增⻓ 19% ⾄ 77.5 亿美元， GAAP 每股收益为
10.57 美元，调整后每股收益为 15.93 美元。
财报公布当⽇，⻉莱德股价⾛出了明显的 “ 冲⾼回落 ” ⾏情，周三早盘⼀度⼤涨  5% ，但随后涨幅

全数收窄，最终平盘收官。不过，买盘在次⽇周四重新发⼒，推动股价强势反弹，收盘⼤涨超
4% 。
净流⼊创纪录， CEO 强调进⼊最强季度
⻉莱德董事⻓兼⾸席执⾏官 Larry Fink 在财报中表⽰，客⼾本季度委托管理的净流⼊规模达 2050
亿美元，过去⼀年累计净流⼊则⾼达 8690 亿美元。他指出：
旗舰 iShares ETF 产品线本季度净吸⾦近 1350 亿美元，成为流⼊的重要来源。公司同期还回购了
5.5 亿美元股票。 Fink 在声明中表⽰：
我们正以加速的势头进⼊季节性最强的第四季度。

私募信贷赎回压⼒边际缓解，但⾏业⻛险仍存
今年以来，私募信贷⾏业整体承压，市场对软件⾏业贷款敞⼝的担忧及⼤规模赎回请求持续困
扰该板块。⻉莱德的私募信贷业务表现出相对抗压性，净流⼊环⽐增加 50 亿美元。
⻉莱德⾸席财务官 Martin Small 在分析师电话会议中表⽰，私募信贷的投资需求整体稳健。他补
充称：
赎回压⼒⽅⾯，⻉莱德企业贷款基⾦的赎回申请占⽐从第⼆季度的 13.3% 降⾄最新季度的约
11.5% ，边际有所缓解。
不过，评级机构穆迪在 10 ⽉ 14 ⽇的报告中警告称，实际情况可能⽐已披露的违约率更为严峻。

穆迪指出：
市场基本⾯强劲，受新技术驱动，企业利润率与盈利动能均持续改善。
我们认为，在整个平台上提供私⼈⾼评级信贷存在更⼤、更持久的机遇，同时也有助于深化客⼾
关系。
在较⾼利率持续维持、再融资困难、赎回压⼒以及透明度不⾜等因素的共同拖累下，美国私募信
贷市场中较弱的中间市场借款⼈资产质量⻛险正在积聚。

Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices to stay  flat MoM in October: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and 75"
TV panel prices are expected to stay flat for the month of October. While panel
makers had previously notified their brands customers of potential price hikes from
4Q26 to mitigate elevated component costs, we believe that negotiations are
ongoing, as TV panel order momentum might slow down in the coming months on

seasonality and TV brands could reduce orders on higher panel prices. On the other
hand, panel makers continue to demonstrate good discipline, since Chinese panel
makers are committed to annual maintenance during the October holiday to control
output. We think it seems more likely that we will see TV panel price hikes toward
late 4Q26 when TV brands start to restock for CNY promotions. Our base-case
assumption is for the TV panel prices  to be flat QoQ in 4Q26, on a quarterly average

basis.
IT panel prices to remain flat MoM in October: Monitor and notebook panel prices
are expected to be flat MoM during the month. Both applications continue to  see
slower demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about
increasing component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and
reluctant to given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect
IT panel prices to remain flat QoQ in 4Q26.

Risk/reward  less attractive: We maintain the view that the current valuation of
Taiwanese panel makers, AUO (2409.TW) at 2.0x P/B and Innolux (3481.TW) at 1.7x
P/B, have already baked in the expectations of glass in advanced packaging for
revenue contribution in 2028. However, some supply-chain players think this might
take longer, and there seem to be more suppliers from different supply chains vying
for this opportunity. In China, while expectations are also building for advanced

packaging, we have a    relative preference for BOE (000725.SZ; 1.4x P/B) over TCL
(000100.SZ; 1.7x P/B), on its scale  and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We believe current valuations of 1.5-2.0x P/B have already priced in expectations
of glass in advanced packaging from 2H28 for the panel makers.
Nonetheless,  the risk in commercialization and competition remain key to watch.

Note: To be more consistent with the industry practice, WitsView changed the way it
publishes panel price quotes in January 2025. It now only provides expectations of the full-
month panel price outlook on the 5th of every month, instead of specific quotes for the first
half of the month. It will then provide finalized prices for the month on the 20th of every
month, which will be similar to the original 2H panel pricing quotes.

Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
TV Panel Prices Trended Down in 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM, August was down 1% MoM, and September was flat MoM,   in line
with our expectation that TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That
said, we do not expect a significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide

downside support.

Below-seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization  ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance
following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.

Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, followed by a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing
structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to

profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural
Changes on Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In, June 17, 2021).

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Largely Fair
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply-chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.6x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.7x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end demand: If end demand for major applications turns out to be better
than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.

壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要: 
 訂單滿載與高能見度：信紘科現有訂單達 230 億元，受惠於
AI 與半導體擴建潮，廠務設備與二次配工程訂單滿載，能見度
看至 2028 年。  
 大型統包與海外擴張：業務從單一工程升級至數十億級大型機
電統包，並結合策略夥伴進軍美、日、德、新等海外市場，帶
動營收成長。  
 綠色永續與持續收益：成立專責子公司銳源環境科技，聚焦廢
液處理與資源循環，將一次性工程建置延伸至維修與代操等持
續性收入。

一. 高科技建廠需求強勁，1H26 稅後 EPS 達 7.70 元 
信紘科以特氣特化廠務供應設備系統與二次配裝機工程為主要業
務，近年營運重心與服務範疇持續升級至二次配統包以及機電統
包工程。2025 年高科技產業營收達 59.45 億元，占整體營收高達 
93.56%；2026 年第二季高科技產業營收仍占絕大多數，半導體與
高科技建廠已成為公司最核心且穩定的營收來源

2026 年上半年營收達 32.19 億元；營業毛利 7.49 億元， 毛利率 
23.25% ；營業利益 3.55 億元，稅後淨利 3.79 億元，累計 EPS 達 
7.70 元。目前公司現有在手訂單高達 230 億元，訂 單能見度已直
達 2028 年，整體營運維持強勁成長動能。

二.半導體擴建為主要成長動能，工程與統包範疇擴大 
在廠務與工程服務部分，信紘科目前量產與工程實績包含特氣特
化廠務供應設備、系統建置及二次配管線裝機工程。為進一步提
升單案規模與營運效能，公司將服務領域延伸至二次配統包與大
型機電統包（GC/EPC），使專案金額邁入數十億級規模。後續業
務擴張重點，亦包含綠色製造與循環經濟，於 2025 年 12 月成
立專責子公司「銳源環境科技」，聚焦廢液處理與資源循環，將
一次性建置工程延伸至維修、營運代操及再生產品銷售等多元商
業模式等長期持續性收入。

目前主要服務據點皆配合客戶全球擴產需求建置，公司在台灣擁
有苗栗、台中、台南等據點，憑藉完整的本土供應鏈與人力調度，
能就近支援各大晶圓廠新建、擴建與改建之即時需求（主系統工

三. 預估 2026 / 2027 年稅後 EPS 14.76 / 16.83 元 
預估 2026 全年營收 71.17 億元，YoY +12.0%；營業毛利 16.41 
億元，YoY +27.1%，毛利率 23.06%；營業利益 7.92 億元，稅後

半導體廠與先進封裝建廠需求熱、在手 230 億元訂單高能見度，
加上大型 GC 統包工程持續認列與海外業務擴張，預計 2027 年
營收 78.29 億元，YoY +10.0%，營業毛利 18.18 億元，毛利率 
23.22% ，營業利益 9.76 億元，稅後純益 8.29 億元，稅後 EPS 為 
16.83 元。

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 330 元 
綜合考量 (1)先進製程相關半導體需求維持強勁 (2) 公司可參與
的工程範圍擴大至海外及機電統包等大型工程，並觀察歷史及同
業本益比區間 ， 目前本益比 16 倍評價偏低 ， 故投資建議為「買進」，
目標價 330 元（20x 2027EPS）。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
信紘科技（Trusval Technology，股票代號：6667）成立於 1995 年，
深耕高科技製造供應鏈布局，並由既有廠務供應設備系統服務，
逐步拓展至機電統包、建廠總承攬及整合型工程解決方案。目前
業務範疇涵蓋廠務設備、製程二次配裝機服務，以及高科技廠房
整合建置與機電統包工程，可依客戶建廠及擴產需求，提供從工
程規劃、設計、採購、施工、測試驗證至系統整合與專案管理的
一站式服務。憑藉跨工種整合、精密製程系統建置及大型專案管
理能力，公司持續提升高附加價值工程案件比重，協助客戶縮短
建廠時程、確保工程品質並提升營運效率。因應全球淨零轉型與
資源循環需求，信紘科技亦積極發展製程機能水設備、廢液處理
與循環經濟解方、資源零廢中心建置等業務，將工程整合能力延
伸至綠色製造與永續基礎建設。

客戶新建、擴建及改建廠需求，加上工程需求穩定且執行週期較
短（主系統約 6 至 8 個月，二次配約 4 至 6 個月），構築了
公司長期穩健的營業基礎。

為打造未來成長新引擎，公司順應客戶海外建廠的全球布局與緊
湊時程，將成熟的工程能力輸出海外，信紘科不僅以既有產物工
程為切入點，逐步擴展至純水、廢水、無塵室及機電等領域以擴
大單一案件服務範疇，在非美日等新興區域更結合策略夥伴共同
承攬；同時，公司統包能力持續升級，從早期高技術門檻的二次
配工程，拓展至跨系統二次配統包，進而邁向承接大規模、高整
合度的跨國 GC 與工程管理角色，透過整合台外資源提升接單層
級與案件金額，全面貼近客戶全球建廠需求。

二.產業趨勢概況 
市場概況 
A.半導體產業概況  
半導體產業為全球數位經濟之核心基礎建設，近年在人工智慧
（AI）、高效能運算（HPC）及邊緣運算應用加速普及帶動下，產
業需求結構出現顯著變化。先進製程與高密度運算晶片需求持續
提升，使晶圓廠投資動能自庫存調整週期中快速復甦，並進入新
一輪資本支出擴張階段。 根據國際半導體產業協會（SEMI）2025
年發布之預測，全球 300mm 晶圓廠設備支出於 2026 年至 2028 年
間累計將達約 3,740 億美元，其中 2025 年單年度支出將首次突破
1,000 億美元水準。顯示全球半導體建廠投資已由短期循環性波
動，逐步轉為結構性成長態勢。另依 Deloitte（2025）預估，全球
半導體銷售額有機會於 2030 年前後突破 1 兆美元規模，反映產業
長期需求動能仍具成長潛力。

B.半導體產業 ESG 趨勢 
隨淨零排放與國際永續規範逐步落實，ESG 已由合規要求轉為半
導體供應鏈的重要競爭要素。依 SNS Insider（ 2025）預估，全球
半導體廢液處理市場將由 2024 年的 27.2 億美元成長至 2032 年的
49.2 億美元，其中亞太地區受惠於晶圓廠持續擴建與永續轉型，
成長動能可望高於全球平均。在此趨勢下，半導體廠務環保技術
逐步朝 「資源再生、能源轉化、源頭減量」三大方向發展。首先，
硫酸、氨氮廢水及有機溶劑等製程廢液，正由傳統末端處理轉向
回收再生，經純化後重新作為工業級或電子級原料使用，提高資
源循環率並降低廢棄物處置需求。其次，有機廢液處理逐步導入
Waste-to-Energy 技術，將廢液轉化為燃料或能源使用， 以降低外購
能源需求與碳排放。

此外，先進製程亦更加重視源頭減量，透過綠色化學品替代與先
進流體製程，降低強酸、強鹼及水資源使用量。隨 3nm 以下製程
持續發展，兼具降低化學品耗用、廢水負荷與營運成本效益的技
術，導入需求可望提升。 
整體而言，未來半導體廠務市場將由單純設備供應，逐步轉向整

Growth in multiples
We forecast revenue to more than double next year
Bloom Energy’s planned capacity expansion of up to 10GW strengthens Kaori’s
long-term SOFC growth outlook, while higher liquid cooling content and
shipments should sustain liquid cooling momentum into 2027. CSP CDU heat
exchangers and semiconductor equipment cooling could provide additional

growth drivers from 4Q26. We raise our 2027/28 EPS forecasts by 14%/12% and
increase our target price to NT$2,710 from NT$2,300. Reiterate HC O-PF rating.
Bloom Energy to double its capacity
Bloom Energy (BE) has announced plans to acquire a 158k sqft facility in F remont,
U
S, comparable in size to its existing Fremont manufacturing plant, which can
produce up to 5GW. We believe the acquisition could support an eventual doubling
of its fuel cell stack manufacturing capacity to 10GW. We expect its supply chain

to support shipments of 2/4/6GW in 2026/27/28 to meet strong solid oxide fuel
cell (SOFC). demand. To support this ramp, Kaori is expanding capacity to 6-7x its
current level by end-2027. We now forecast Kaori’s sales from BE to grow by
185%/66% YoY in 2027/28, versus our previous estimate of 101%/77%.
Strong liquid cooling momentum into 2027
With SpaceX planning an additional 6-8GW of datacentre capacity in 2027, we
b
elieve GPU server suppliers Supermicro and Dell will be key beneficiaries,

supporting continued growth across their supply chains from 2H26 into 2027.
Higher content value for Rubin manifolds, together with stronger CDU and rear-
door heat exchanger (RDHx) shipments, should support Kaori’s liquid cooling
momentum. We forecast its liquid cooling revenue to grow by 60%/25% YoY in
2027/28.
Diversifying the growth engines
While SOFC and Supermicro-related liquid cooling remain Kaori’s main growth
d
rivers, we believe plate heat exchangers for CSP CDUs and liquid cooling

applications in semiconductor equipment could emerge as additional growth drivers
from 4Q26. These opportunities should broaden Kaori’s customer and application
exposure, supporting its longer-term growth outlook.
Earnings revisions and valuation
We raise our 2027/28SP EPS forecasts by 14%/12% to reflect higher expected
d
emand and shipments for BE's fuel cells, alongside a stronger liquid cooling
outlook. We increase our TP to NT$2,710 from NT$2,300, based on an unchanged

40x 2H27-1H28SP PE. Reiterate High Conviction Outperform rating.

Investment thesis
Kaori is well positioned to capitalise on rising demand for liquid cooling, and it
expects related product shipments to ramp up. The fuel-cell business should be a
huge growth driver in 2026-27 to meet AI power infrastructure demand in the US.
In the longer term, we believe the company will also benefit from heat-pump
demand as Europe is phasing out gas boilers, despite some noise about near-term
demand. Kaori has been planning capacity expansion earlier than peers to fully

capture demand, with rising exposure to large clients. We thus expect the company
to enjoy robust earnings in coming years.

### Page 5

Growth in multiples Kaori - HC O-PF
6 October 2026 5
Catalysts
The major growth catalysts largely depend on fuel-cell projects for AI datacentres
taking off, increasing content value in liquid cooling offerings, and a clearer outlook
for hydrogen electrolyzer development.
Valuation details
We adopt a PE-based valuation methodology as we believe the market will mainly
focus on the earnings growth outlook, which is better reflected with PE metrics.

We base our valuation on 40x PE, which is around +1sd of its five-year average and
supported by an 93% EPS Cagr in 2026-28SP, to account for the surging demand
for AI server liquid cooling and power infrastructure. We are positive on the
company's long-term outlook given its growing stationary fuel cell demand from AI
datacentres, as well as its efforts to diversify customer exposure for liquid cooling
business. We believe the company can maintain its competitive edge over other

cooling players given its expertise in vacuum welding technology to manufacture
high-quality products.
Investment risks
Downside risks: 1) increased competition for Bloom Energy's fuel cell business or
new technology solutions replacing fuel cells; 2) slower kick-off of new fuel cell
projects for AI datacentres; 3) weaker-than-expected liquid cooling adoption; 4)
slower rollout of Europe/US government subsidies for heat pump installations.

### Page 1

현대차 (005380)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.16  
4분기 반등 기대

3Q26 Preview 
현대차는 3Q26E 매출액 42.4조원(-9.4% YoY, -13.9% QoQ), 영업이익 2.5조원(-
2.8% YoY, -13.5% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 각 부문
별 영업이익은 자동차 1.7조원(-2.0% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 4.8%), 금융 5,501억
원(-4.5% YoY, -27.5% QoQ, OPM 6.9%)으로 예상한다. 
생산 정상화 및 신차 확대를 통한 수익성 개선 기대 
현대차는 글로벌 도매 판매 91.8만대(-11.5% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 90.9
만대(-9.6%)를 기록했다. 7~8월에 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생산 차
질이 발생했다. 파업 영향으로 HEV 판매 증가율이 1%에 그치면서, HEV 판매 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선 효과도 시장 기대에 미치지 못했다.  
4분기부터는 3분기 파업 영향으로 지연된 생산량 회복 및 신차 확대가 본격화되면서, 
전분기 대비 매출 및 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 동사는 보급형 전기차 아이오닉3의 튀
르키예 생산을 9월부터 시작했으며, 이를 통해 유럽 시장 판매량은 점차 회복될 전망
이다. 또한, 1) HMGMA를 통한 HEV 미국 현지 생산 확대, 2) 제네시스 HEV 모델 순
차적 출시를 통해 내년부터 북미를 중심으로 믹스 개선이 본격화될 전망이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 520,000원으로 하향 
2027년 영업이익은 11.2조원(+10.2%)를 기록할 것으로 예상된다. 원화 약세 지속을 
반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다. 다만, 현재 주가는 
12M Fwd P/E 기준 6.1배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 수준이다. 1) RMAC 
성과 구체화, 2) RA(로봇 생산법인) 착공 등 Physical AI 사업이 구체화될 경우 밸류에
이션 할증이 가능할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

목표주가 (하향) 520,000 원 
현재주가 295,500 원

## Document 147: Advanced economies _ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-09-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Advanced economies  _ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-09-24).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

3
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
History tends to repeat itself in advanced economies. Once again, growth ultimately fell short of expectations by only a small
margin in the first half of 2026, despite the conflict in Iran. As early as 2025, the impact of tariffs was less severe than feared.
This is a sign that structural factors (notably AI, defence and electrification) are underpinning growth in these countries

and are expected to continue to do so in 2027. However, there are significant risks to the economic outlook, primarily of a
geopolitical nature, with a significant upside risk to inflation and interest rates. The other instance of history repeating itself
concerns a period that those under 20 cannot have experienced: the period leading up to the 2008 crisis, echoes of which can
be seen in terms of growth, inflation, the direction of monetary policy, and long-term interest rates.

Advanced economies are experiencing trends similar to those they
faced nearly 20 years ago:
- Growth remains resilient in 2025-26 despite shocks, as it did in 2006-
07, in the major advanced economies.
- Inflation is above central banks’ targets: as in 2006-07, this calls for a
tightening of monetary policy.
- In Europe and the United States, long-term interest rates have re -
turned to their 2006-07 levels (1996 in Japan): a challenging environ-

ment for public finances in a context where public debt ratios are signi-
ficantly higher than those seen 20 years ago.
THE GLOBAL ECONOMY IN THE MIDST OF A GROWTH CYCLE
We are already well into 2026, and the picture so far is clear: glo -
bal growth is proving resilient. Following a US tariff shock last year,
growth is once again proving, for the time being, to be significantly less
affected by the conflict in Iran than had been anticipated when it first

broke out. Initially, however, there were fears that this new shock would
hit growth even harder. As with the tariff hike in 2025, the impact was
measurable, particularly in terms of consumer confidence. But in both
2025 and 2026, growth has also shown a degree of resilience.
The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise
in oil prices, the recessionary impact of which is not yet apparent.
According to our forecasts, growth was expected to reach or exceed 1%

in 2026 in the four major advanced economies (the Eurozone, Japan,
the United Kingdom and the United States, where it exceeds 2%) for
the second consecutive year. This had not happened (excluding the
post-COVID catch-up period) since 2006-07. Growth figures are more
moderate (particularly for demographic reasons), but the private sec -
tor is not (at present) showing the same excesses (at that time, the
credit boom had preceded the worst crisis since the 1930s); although

debt levels have recently risen in the US tech sector.
The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial
intelligence (AI). It is fuelling global trade and business investment
in most countries. This is clearly the case in the United States, where
business investment is growing strongly as investment plans in the
US tech sector are implemented.
However, the world’s major economies are currently engaged in a
broader push to expand their capital goods investment. Alongside the

ongoing development of services—particularly digital services—we
have seen a rebound in industrial production in most countries since
early 2025. The leading sectors are primarily technology equipment,
machinery, aeronautics, defence and their essential inputs (energy and
metals). This trend is evident everywhere, including in Europe, and
is becoming entrenched over the long term, with an improvement in
business climate indicators, particularly new export orders. Growth is

therefore expected to strengthen further in 2027, particularly in the
Eurozone, where German investment plans are set to gather momen -
tum and whose spillover effects will have a greater impact on the eco-
nomy.
However, not all sectors are experiencing the same momentum. This
is particularly true for the sectors that depend on household consump-
tion, which has shown mixed trends. Our observation that consumer
spending is more buoyant in the United States than in Europe was

confirmed in 2026, and the outlook does not point to a change in this
ranking.
EDITORIAL
RESILIENCE TO SHOCK AFTER SHOCK

### Page 2

4
EcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The bank
for a changing
world
While European and US households are experiencing more or less the
same energy price shock, inflation remains higher in the United States.
But this has not prevented consumer spending from keeping pace, pri-
marily because it is driven by the wealthiest households, who are less
sensitive to inflation than to the wealth effect. Furthermore, while the

savings rate remains high in the Eurozone, US households have been
drawing down their savings. This is also true of British households,
which explains why the UK has seen slightly stronger growth since
the beginning of the year. In Japan, government spending has partially
offset energy inflation (while other countries are doing so to a much
lesser extent than in 2022), which has supported consumption growth.
However, these buffers—reduced savings or fiscal support—have their

limits when shocks persist. The two major current conflicts (Iran and
Ukraine) are far from being resolved, and their impact is continuing.
This persistent geopolitical risk and its implications for inflation repre-
sent a significant headwind to the growth outlook for 2027.
HIGHER INFLATION CALLS FOR MONETARY TIGHTENING
Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks
examined in this publication, which is another similarity (albeit with

different underlying causes) with the 2006-07 period. Japan is the ex -
ception, as government support measures for households are tempo -
rarily holding back inflation.
Apart from the Bank of Japan, which is engaged in a long-term norma-
lisation process, no central bank was expected to raise its key rates
in 2026. But they are now being prompted to do so for reasons that
vary from one region to another. The first is the conflict in Iran and
the resulting rise in oil prices, which triggered a rebound in energy

inflation from the second quarter onwards. However, since the conflict
was not resolved by the memorandum of understanding signed in June,
and given that the subsequent drop in oil prices proved short-lived,
inflation is expected to rise again. The rise in gas prices is expected to
exacerbate the impact, particularly in Europe, where inventories are
being replenished ahead of winter. Electricity prices are expected to
follow suit in most countries, but at a more moderate pace than in

2022. Indeed, gas is now used less to generate electricity than it was
at that time (thanks to the expansion of renewable energy), and to an
even lesser extent in France.
There is a significant risk that energy inflation will spread further to
other components of inflation in the future, and inflation expectations
could eventually reflect this. This is why the European Central Bank
has already raised its key interest rate twice (in June and September)

and, according to our scenario, will likely do so again in December. For
the same reason, we expect the Bank of England to raise its policy rate
in November. Although the cap on energy prices was maintained for
a long time, it has now been raised. As a result, inflation is expected
to rise between now and the end of the year. The aim will then be to
prevent second-round effects.
Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength. In the

United States, it has exceeded the 2% target since 2021. The main
argument in favour of a new cycle of rate hikes (following the one in
2022-2023) lies in the current resilience of growth and the improving
labour market. The Federal Reserve (Fed) must balance two equally
important objectives: maximum employment and price stability. Howe-
ver, unlike in the 4 th quarter of 2025, when the decline in job crea -
tion justified monetary easing (by 75 basis points), the current sharp

rebound in job creation completely reverses that analysis. According
to our forecasts, the unemployment rate is expected to decrease in
the coming months, which pushes back the prospect of a decline in
core inflation. This situation persists due to both supply constraints
(over which a central bank has no control) and excess demand (which
the Fed can, however, mitigate). Consequently, the Fed raised its key
rate in September and is expected to implement two further rate hikes

between now and January 2027.
In Japan, finally, while headline inflation has been brought down by
government support measures, core inflation remains significant.
Wage pressures are mounting against a backdrop of labour shortages.
The Bank of Japan is therefore being forced to accelerate the pace of its
monetary normalisation.
In all these regions, inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026
or Q1 2027. It is against this backdrop of temporary inflation that rate

hikes could also come to an end by that time (excluding Japan). Howe-
ver, this assumes that once the energy shock—the course of which
remains uncertain—subsides, inflation will fall. The effects of El Niño,
which are still difficult to gauge at present, could take over. Inflation
would then remain above the central banks’ target for a longer period.
Under this scenario, rate hikes would go further than is currently en -
visaged.
THE RISE IN LONG- TERM INTEREST RATES: A FACTOR THAT WILL WEIGH

ON PUBLIC FINANCES
Monetary policies have also to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal
policy, particularly in the United States (where the budget deficit is
high and shows no signs of shrinking) and in Japan (where the new
government is pursuing pro-growth policies). In Europe, fiscal policy
is playing a much smaller role than it did in 2022. While the stimulus
measures implemented at that time did help reduce the inflation rate
in the short term (by nearly 2.5 pp in France, for example), by keeping

demand at a high level, they delayed disinflation and increased the
scale of the monetary tightening required.
The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: it triggered a
rise in long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the
start of the year) to levels close to those of 2006-07. This rise is wides-
pread, but its causes vary. Inflation expectations appear to play a more
significant role in the Eurozone, whereas the rise in real interest rates

seems to be the main factor in the United States and Japan.
SOURCE: LSEG, BNP PARIBASCHART 3

### Page 3

5
EcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The bank
for a changing
world
Higher inflation and more resilient real growth than feared point in
the same direction: stronger than expected nominal growth that is li -
kely to be sustained. However, in the long term, high nominal growth
tends to drive up nominal interest rates (as it is a positive sign of more
profitable investment projects), in contrast to the problem of secular

stagnation raised in the 2010s (which combined low nominal growth
with low nominal interest rates).
The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period
of growth), with various situations. In several countries, the public
debt-to-GDP ratio continues to rise. In the United States, deficit reduc-
tion does not appear to be a priority, to the extent that it is expected
to remain above 6% of GDP at the very least until the end of Donald

Trump’s term of office (with little hope that the mid-term elections will
significantly alter this trend). In the United Kingdom and France, fiscal
consolidation has begun, but it is being hampered by the simultaneous
increase in certain areas of expenditure (debt servicing, defence and
healthcare, due in particular to an ageing population), which is slowing
the pace of consolidation. In Germany, the implementation of invest -
ment plans is leading to a widening of the deficit. While this does not

pose a sustainability problem, it does result in an increase in annual
debt issuance. In Japan, the budget deficit is kept in check by interest
payments that remain low, as the rise in interest rates is recent and
the average maturity of the debt is high (10 years). However, expansio-
nary fiscal policy could pose further problems in terms of debt dyna -
mics if it were to continue through the end of the decade. In Italy and
Spain, fiscal consolidation has been sufficient to stabilise the public

debt-to-GDP ratio (and even to reduce it in Spain), but the high level of
Italy’s debt service it vulnerable to a rise in interest rates.
In all these countries, the fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it
will be in 2030. Indeed, in most cases, only half of the debt was is -
sued after 2022 (that is, after the end of the zero-interest-rate period).
The debt service has so far only partially risen, except in the United
States, where most of the increase has already occurred due to a shor-

ter average debt maturity (6 years). This means that, for all countries
combined, the rate of GDP growth remains higher than the average
interest rate paid on public debt (or the effective interest rate).
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with
GDP growth. As a result, in most cases, the debt service will have re -
turned to the level as a share of GDP seen in the early 2000s. In order to
stabilise public debt-to-GDP ratios, primary balances will need to have

improved. This will even require the generation of primary surpluses,
particularly in Italy (which is already achieving this), France and the
United Kingdom. This is a crucial shift that governments will need to
introduce as early as the 2027 budget; otherwise, they risk postponing
the stabilisation of their public debt-to-GDP ratio once again.
Article completed on 17 September 2026
Stéphane Colliac
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

## Document 175: August CPI – Came in on our expectations (2026-10-17)

Source PDF: all report pdf/August CPI – Came in on our expectations (2026-10-17).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-17
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#inflation #australia #rba #economicbulletinsaustralia #inflation
August CPI – Came in on our expectations
By Justin Smirk & Neha Sharma
15:27 September 30 2026 Share
August Monthly CPI: Headline 0.4%mth/4.0%yr, Trimmed Mean 0.2%mth/3.6%yr. Market
services ex volatile –0.4%mth/3.3%yr.

The CPI rose 0.4% in August, spot on Westpac’s expectation but a bit softer than market
expectations of 0.5%. Due to base effects the annual pace picked up to 4.0%yr from 3.5%yr.

### Page 2

Key factors driving the headline result were auto fuels (14.8%); games, toys & hobbies (10.2%) with
rents providing a solid base (0.3%) while some offset was provided by both domestic (–4.9%) and
international holiday travel & accommodation (–1.5%).
Trimmed mean (TM) inflation printed 0.2% again bang on Westpac’s expectation while the market
was slightly higher at 0.3%. The annual pace was flat at 3.6%yr, the third month in a row at this pace,
while the six-month annualise pace was steady at 3.9%yr.

Key factors driving the TM print were: rents (0.3%) and new dwelling purchases (0.2%). The TM
distribution ranged from –0.38% to 0.82%.
Today’s inflation update revealed price discounting for some durable goods as retail demand softens.
We would expect to see a bounce from these categories in the following month. There was also
offsetting strength elsewhere in durable good so at least for now, the momentum continues to
suggest some near term upside risk to our current inflation estimates.

Nevertheless, as the TM came in on expectations and based on a first quick pass at updating the
quarterly profile, we see little pressure to revise our current Q3 estimates of 1.3%qtr for the CPI and
0.9%qtr for the TM.
The RBA remains alert to near term inflationary risks. We are expecting the momentum in TM
inflation to remain well above the top of the target band through to year-end underpinning our
expectations for the RBA to raise rates again in November.

### Page 3

August CPI in more detail
Following the bumper read in July, the monthly CPI moderated in August rising 0.4%mth. This was in
line with our expectations and slightly softer than the market consensus. This took the year-ended pace
to 4.0%yr. On a seasonally adjusted basis the CPI rose 0.7%mth highlighting the impact of seasonal
sales in August.

They key drivers behind the monthly lift were automotive fuel, up 14.8%mth, contributing 0.5ppts to the
headline figure. The surge resulted from the ending of the fuel excise discount on August 3 and rising
global energy prices. With crude oil prices still elevated due to the conflict in the middle east, and
growing shortages of refined products due to both the middle east conflict and Ukrainian assaults on
Russian refining capacity, auto fuel is expected to remain an inflationary impulse at least until the end
of 2026.

The monthly TM also softened, rising 0.2%mth compared to the 0.5%mth read from July. The result
was again in line with our expectations, and very close at two decimal places (0.24%mth vs
0.23%mth), but softer than the market's view. The year-ended pace held at 3.6%yr for the third
consecutive month, although it continued to ease marginally at the second decimal place. In contrast,
the six-month annualised pace has continued to trend higher since April and is currently running at
3.9%yr.

### Page 4

There is a possibility that as the ABS continues to grapple with seasonal adjustment issues we will see
revisions to the TM estimates. Last July also saw a strong 0.48%mth read which eased to 0.29%mth in
August. This is not too dissimilar from the 0.48%mth and 0.24%mth reads for 2026.

We are expecting core inflation to start to moderate as we get to year end but our current estimate for
the month of December still has the TM running at a 3.3% in the year while the six-month annualised
pace is still a chunky 3.1%. Applying a trim on the seasonally adjusted 3m/3m expenditure classes
yields a TM of 0.9%qtr currently. This is in line with our current Q3 TM estimate.

Other measures of underlying inflation told a similar story, easing on a monthly basis but holding steady
on a year-ended basis. Market services ex volatiles fell –0.4%mth to be 3.3%yr higher. Excluding both
volatiles & holidays saw a 0.2%mth/4.1%yr lift.

Most market services components eased in the month (including on a seasonally adjusted basis) but
annual growth across this group as a whole remained above the RBA's 2-3% target band. The near-
term momentum (three-month annualised) is also quite strong across several components of services.
Of note include deposit and loan facilities (11.2%mth), sports participation (10.9%), clothing repair
(7.0%), restaurant meals (6.8%) and other household services (5.2%). We are closely watching market
services as this is where inflationary risks, outside of energy, appear to be most persistent.

Due to its sizable weight in the CPI, and the fact price changes here tend to be less volatile than many
other categories, so they almost always appear in the TM estimates, housing prices remain a key
focus. Rents rose 0.3%mth in August, in line with our expectations, to be up 3.6%yr. Since April, the
seasonally adjusted rents series has been running lower than the non-seasonally adjusted series. We
are expecting rents to hold around this pace till year end before moderating modestly as we move
through 2027.

New dwellings inflation was 0.2%mth in August (softer than our expectation for a 0.4%mth rise). Since
peaking in May, monthly price growth has slowed in each successive month, potentially reflecting
increasing difficulty for developers in passing through higher costs amid softer conditions in established
housing markets and weaker demand in the face of higher interest rates. The annual pace looks to
have topped out. It was at 5.8%yr in June easing to 5.4%yr currently with the three-month annualised
pace at 3.7%. We expect dwelling price inflation to continue easing through to the end of 2027 as
falling established house prices limit the ability of builders to pass on rising construction costs. There is
a medium-term risk that as conditions improve builders may raise prices more aggressively to claw
back margins.

### Page 5

Overall, the breadth of inflation moderated in August. The share of the basket running above 3%yr
eased from 45% to 42%, with the share below 2%yr increasing from 38% to 41%.

### Page 6

Key surprises in the month:
Upside surprises

Downside surprises
Insurance surprised to the upside (0.7%mth vs 0.3%mth) after a run of weaker-than-usual reads over
the past two months.
Motor vehicle repair costs rose a solid 0.8% against our 0.3%mth expectations, with spare parts and
accessories also up 0.3%mth vs –0.4%mth.
Footwear rose 1.7%mth against our expectations for a -2.9%mth decline, the gains were broad-
based across women, men & children.
Household goods surprised to the upside. Household appliances, utensils and tools rose 1.9%mth
against our flat expectations, meanwhile household textiles fell –1.0%mth against our expectations
for a –5.0%mth fall. Furniture and furnishings also fell by less than expected –0.6%mth vs –1.3%mth.
Recreation and culture surprised to the upside (–1.0%mth vs –1.5%mth), owing to a 1.4%mth lift in
other recreation, sport & culture against our expectations for a flat read on the back of an outsized
gain in games, toys & hobbies (10.2%mth vs 2.5%mth). Holiday travel was marginally less weak than
expected (–3.3% vs –3.5%mth).

### Page 7

Food was the key downside miss falling –0.1%mth against our expectations for a 0.2%mth lift. Meat
& seafood (–0.9%mth vs 0.5%mth) and fruit & vegetables (–1.2%mth vs 0.1%mth) were the key
areas of weakness.
Audio, visual and computing equipment and services came in weaker-than-expected (0.7%mth vs
1.2%mth), owing to a softer 0.6%mth lift in computing services (3.0%mth expected). This was partly
balanced by a stronger than expected lift in computing equipment (0.9%mth vs –0.5%mth). The latter

is where we are most likely to see the impact of the AI boom in the CPI, due to computer chip
inflation, other than construction cost inflation for new dwellings.
Motor vehicles came in lower at –0.4%mth vs –0.1%mth which could be the start of price pressures
associated with the rapid expansion of the share of Chinese cars in the Australian market.
Automotive fuel was also slightly softer at 14.8%mth vs 16.7%mth.
Garments and accessories were both softer. Garments fell –1.9%mth against our expectations for a

0.2%mth rise, meanwhile accessories came in at 0.1%mth vs 0.5%mth expected.

### Page 8

What does this mean for the RBA?
Yesterday, post the RBA rate hike and monetary policy decision statement, Westpac changed its view
from a November follow up rate hike being a risk to being the base case. The post-meeting statement
was notable for its emphasis on the Middle East conflict, noting that “global energy prices are now
much higher than had been assumed in the August forecasts”. In addition, RBA’s liaison suggests firms
are passing this on or planning to do so. The pass-through was characterised as partial, and additional

to domestically driven inflation arising from capacity pressures. We read this as implying that the RBA
is concerned about upside risks to underlying inflation in Q4 of this year, as well as Q3.

### Page 9

Related articles
Our current forecast of 0.9%qtr for the September quarter TM stands post today’s release and
represents a step up from 0.8% in the June quarter holding the annual pace at 3.6%yr. Our current
forecast for the Q4 TM is 0.7%qtr/3.3%yr but given the move in energy prices, and anecdotes of firms
now passing on those rising costs, we too see upside risks to this estimate.

As such, we hold to the view that the RBA will follow up with another rate hike in November. For more
information on the RBA decision please see Luci Ellis’s note “RBA hikes in September, November
follow-up now expected".

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên khung tuần, xuyên thủng vùng hỗ trợ 
gần quanh 1750, cho thấy áp lực bán đã có phần quyết liệt hơn sau 2 tuần giằng co trước 
đó. Trên khung ngày, sau các phiên lình xình vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng dần chuyển sang 
diễn biến giảm điểm rõ nét hơn về cuối tuần, đồng thời cắt sâu xuống dưới SMA50, thể 
hiện áp lực phân phối đang có phần lấn át mặc dù chưa xảy ra hiện tượng bán tháo khi 
khối lượng khớp lệnh được giữ ở mức thấp. Diễn biến tính từ vùng đỉnh 1870 vào đầu tháng 
9 đang không mấy khả quan, tuy nhiên xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn, tính từ đáy 1640 vào cuối 
tháng 7, hiện vẫn đang được bảo lưu và VNINDEX được kỳ vọng sẽ có nhịp hồi phục trở lại 
khi về đến vùng hỗ trợ kế tiếp tại quanh 1710. 
Kháng cự xa: 1820 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1775 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1710 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1670 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

## Document 218: CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng Không Taseco - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng Không Taseco - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-22).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Company)

### Page 1

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco (AST) 
Tiếp tục mở rộng điểm kinh doanh 
22/09/2026  
Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD tăng trưởng tích cực trong 
2Q2026

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu

Mở rộng mạng lưới tạo động lực tăng 
trưởng doanh thu mới

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ cải thiện biên lợi nhuận

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá mục 
tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), với 
động lực chủ yếu đến từ (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn 
Nhất và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ VND 
(+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa tại các thị trường AST khai thác 
tăng bình quân 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. Lưu lượng và tỷ trọng khách quốc tế 
gia tăng được kỳ vọng thúc đẩy số giao dịch và doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

Chúng tôi dự phóng số điểm kinh doanh AST tăng từ 145 điểm cuối năm 2026 lên 
182 điểm năm 2030. Sau đóng góp từ 19 điểm tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất và 6 điểm mới tại 
T2 Nội Bài từ 2025 - 2026, các dự án Long Thành, Phú Quốc và Gia Bình tiếp tục mở 
ra dư địa mở rộng mạng lưới trong trung và dài hạn cho doanh nghiệp.

Việc mở rộng các hoạt động mang lại lợi nhuận cao, bao gồm hàng miễn thuế, 
phòng chờ thương gia và tối ưu hoạt động tại các điểm kinh doanh được kỳ vọng 
tác động tích cực lên biên lợi nhuận của AST. Chúng tôi dự phóng biên lợi nhuận 
gộp đạt 64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027, hỗ trợ LNST công ty mẹ tăng lần lượt 
48%/17% yoy.

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với AST, giá mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 21/09/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco là một 
trong những doanh nghiệp có quy mô mạng 
lưới điểm kinh doanh lớn trong lĩnh vực dịch 
vụ phi hàng không tại Việt Nam, với 141 
điểm kinh doanh tại 6 sân bay quốc tế tính 
đến cuối tháng 6/2026. Hoạt động cốt lõi của 
công ty bao gồm bán lẻ bách hóa lưu niệm 
(BHLN), hàng miễn thuế, dịch vụ ăn uống và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Ngoài ra, AST còn 
tham gia lĩnh vực suất ăn hàng không thông 
qua khoản đầu tư tại công ty liên kết VinaCS. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích

11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

điểm và tăng lên 182 điểm vào 2030.

phòng chờ và tối ưu vận hành.

KQKD tăng trưởng tích cực trong 
2Q2026

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), 
với động lực: (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ 
VND (+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Biểu đồ 5. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh của AST

Nguồn:AST

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng tăng trưởng lưu lượng hành khách, đặc biệt là sự gia tăng tỷ 
trọng khách quốc tế, sẽ thúc đẩy doanh thu và hiệu quả kinh doanh của AST 
trong giai đoạn 2026–2030. Theo Cục Hàng không Việt Nam, lượng khách thông 
qua các cảng hàng không trong 7T2026 đạt hơn 76 triệu lượt (+7% yoy), trong 
đó khách quốc tế đạt gần 30 triệu lượt (+11% yoy), trong khi khách nội địa ghi 
nhận tăng trưởng 4% yoy. Xu hướng này được hỗ trợ bởi nhu cầu du lịch quốc 
tế, chính sách thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng bay, trong khi tăng 
trưởng kinh tế và thu nhập tiếp tục tạo nền tảng cho nhu cầu đi lại trong nước. 
Trên cơ sở đó, chúng tôi dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế và nội địa tại các thị 
trường AST khai thác tăng bình quân lần lượt 11% và 5%/năm đến 2030, qua đó 
nâng dần tỷ trọng khách quốc tế trong tổng lưu lượng, thúc đẩy doanh 
thu/điểm kinh doanh nhờ: (1) lưu lượng hành khách qua cảng hàng không gia 
tăng và (2) tỷ trọng khách quốc tế cao hơn hỗ trợ doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

 Biểu đồ 7. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh AST (tỷ VND)

Nguồn: AST  Nguồn: AST

Mở rộng mạng lưới tại các nhà ga 
hiện hữu và sân bay mới, gia tăng 
quy mô kinh doanh đến 2030 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng chu kỳ mở rộng hạ tầng hàng không giai đoạn 2026–2030 sẽ 
tạo dư địa gia tăng quy mô kinh doanh cho AST, với số điểm kinh doanh dự 
phóng đạt 145 điểm vào cuối 2026 và tăng lên 182 điểm vào năm 2030. Cụ thể: 
- Trong ngắn hạn, tăng trưởng năm 2026 được hỗ trợ bởi việc ghi nhận 
đủ năm doanh thu từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
trong năm 2025, cùng 6 điểm mới tại T2 Nội Bài trong năm 2026, trong 
đó có 2 cửa hàng miễn thuế và 2 cửa hàng Memories of Vietnam khai 
trương trong 2Q2026. 
- Trong trung và dài hạn, các dự án Cảng HKQT Long Thành, Cảng HKQT 
Phú Quốc nâng cấp mở rộng và Cảng HKQT Gia Bình sẽ góp phần thúc 
đẩy ngành hàng không Việt Nam nói chung, bổ sung mặt bằng thương 
mại, mở ra cơ hội phát triển các mảng kinh doanh cốt lõi cho AST nói 
riêng bao gồm các mảng bán lẻ, miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Với kinh nghiệm khai thác 141 điểm kinh doanh 
tại 6 sân bay quốc tế và lịch sử trúng thầu tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất, T2 Nội 
Bài trong thời gian qua, chúng tôi kỳ vọng AST có khả năng cạnh tranh 
cao trong việc đấu thầu quyền khai thác mặt bằng tại các sân bay mới, 
đặc biệt là Gia Bình, qua đó duy trì vị thế và thị phần kinh doanh phi 
hàng không tại khu vực phía Bắc khi một phần lưu lượng hành khách 
được phân bổ lại từ Nội Bài.

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ mở rộng biên lợi nhuận  
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng biên lợi nhuận của AST tiếp tục cải thiện trong 2026 - 2027, 
nhờ định hướng mở rộng các mảng có biên lợi nhuận tốt như hàng miễn thuế 
và phòng chờ thương gia, kết hợp tối ưu hiệu quả vận hành tại các điểm kinh 
doanh. Cụ thể: (1) AST ưu tiên gia tăng số điểm kinh doanh hàng miễn thuế 
nhằm đón đầu tăng trưởng nhanh và ổn định của lượng khách quốc tế, qua đó 
nâng đóng góp lợi nhuận từ hoạt động mua sắm tại sân bay, (2) mở rộng mạng 
lưới phòng chờ, đồng thời gia tăng lượng khách sử dụng trên nền năng lực phục 
vụ hiện hữu và (3) tối ưu hóa hiệu quả hoạt động thông qua cải thiện bố trí mặt 
bằng, danh mục sản phẩm và kiểm soát chi phí tại các điểm kinh doanh, qua 
đó tăng doanh thu và lợi nhuận thu được trên mỗi khách hàng.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá 
mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu AST, với giá mục tiêu 76,500 
VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 
21/09/2026, dựa trên triển vọng tăng trưởng từ lượng khách quốc tế, mở rộng 
mạng lưới điểm kinh doanh và khả năng cải thiện biên lợi nhuận của doanh 
nghiệp. Chúng tôi sử dụng phương pháp định giá từng phần (SOTP), bao gồm 
DCF cho hoạt động kinh doanh cốt lõi của AST và P/E mục tiêu 11x đối với phần 
lợi nhuận thuộc sở hữu AST tại VinaCS. Các giả định chính gồm: (1) lượng khách 
quốc tế/nội địa tăng trung bình 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030, (2) số điểm kinh 
doanh tăng từ 145 điểm cuối 2026 lên 182 điểm năm 2030, với giả định AST 
trúng thầu mặt bằng tại cả Long Thành và Gia Bình và (3) biên lợi nhuận gộp 
2026/2027 đạt 64.7/65.5%. Đối với VinaCS, chúng tôi kỳ vọng nhà máy suất ăn 
Long Thành sẽ hỗ trợ tăng trưởng lợi nhuận trong trung và dài hạn khi dự kiến 
có thể bắt đầu đóng góp lợi nhuận cho AST từ 2028.

Rủi ro đối với định giá bao gồm: (1) AST không giành được quyền khai thác tại 
Long Thành và Gia Bình như kỳ vọng, (2) lưu lượng khách dịch chuyển giữa các 
sân bay mới và hiện hữu khiến doanh thu tại một số điểm kinh doanh bị sụt 
giảm, (3) cạnh tranh gia tăng từ các doanh nghiệp kinh doanh dịch vụ phi hàng 
không khác và (4) căng thẳng địa chính trị kéo dài, giá dầu tăng gây áp lực lên 
hoạt động hàng không và chi tiêu của hành khách.

## Document 307: Sales Trader Commentary_ TAIEX 49k Wobble - July De Ja Vu, or Just Pausing Before the Santa Rally_ _ GSTW

Source PDF: all report pdf/Sales Trader Commentary_ TAIEX 49k Wobble - July De Ja Vu, or Just Pausing Before the Santa Rally_ _ GSTW.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Sales Trader Commentary: TAIEX 49k Wobble -
July De Ja Vu, or Just Pausing Before the Santa
Rally? | GSTW
13 October 2026 | 4:11 PM Taiwan Standard Time
Robert Wang
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. Taipei Branch
FICC & Equities
Index lost the 49k points threshold and saw the biggest decline on a point basis since
the end of July. Market seemed to lose the steam to break through 50k points after
the past choppy sessions. Is this July de ja vu or some healthy pullback? Foreigners

and domestic investors seem to share different narrative. Check out below for our
investor feedback.
Key Investor Feedback and Observations
Positioning. we saw meaningful outflows today from all kinds of investors today,
again focusing in ex-TSMC large caps. Today foreigners net sold US$5.2bn today,
the second largest in the history (simultaneously drove TWD weakness over 32.23
today). Domestic investors, especially retail, on the other hand, anchored the

market with the record high margin loan balance (as of 10/12/2026). Local funds
remained buyer as well mainly thanks to ETFs inflow.
Macro. CPI headline brought uncertainty and forced global investors to turn
cautious, which was exacerbated by high yields and crude. For Taiwan, AI trade is
relatively resilient to the Middle Eastern tension and high energy prices. However,
at some point, it is going to have a real economic impact either through Fed or

some other macro data.
Sentiment. Surprisingly, people are still bullish in Taiwan, though they also
acknowledge some choppy trading sessions ahead. Local investor feedback is not
calling for capitulation; in fact, most believe this is just another "two-steps forward
one-step backward" cadence towards another record high. Nonetheless, some
retail start taking chips off the table. This echoes our observation among the
foreign investors. Yes, the AI fundamental future is all sunshine, but as we head into

the end of year, it is natural for them to protect their great fun thus far this year
instead of adding on immediate risk. I'd imagine the next few weeks are going to be
full of 1) rapid sector rotation; 2) weak or unsustainable momentum before Santa
comes into town after the midterm election for our year-end rally Christmas gift.
TSMC Earnings and Others. We know the numbers are going to be good, and we
also know people are going to sell-on-facts after TSMC earnings call; recall most

recent one happened right after the meeting that scarred a lot of investors since.
Perhaps this time people started trimming ahead (and maybe too early?), which
provides some window for a quick rebound into small and mid cap. OCP summit is
happening right now, and TPCA Show will start next week. Watch out for single
stock movement mentioned in the conferences.
10/13/26, 4:13 PM Sales Trader Commentary: TAIEX 49k Wobble - July De Ja Vu, or Just Pausing Before the Santa Rally? | GSTW

https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/812c043a-8535-4003-887e-b01f79096974 1/8

### Page 2

Sector. 1) ABF/CCL is still the crowded long sectors for both foreign and domestic
investor, but echoing on above points, investors need more catalysts to add on
risks or drive the re-ratings. 2) Power is another big issues that data centers need
to resolve, power solutions and fuel cells name are also key focus in Taiwan. 3) Let's
not forget about OSATs. We need more equipment and testing capacity to suffice
enlarging demand from both ASIC and GPU.

Key Stocks I Follow: Unimicron, Kinsus, NYPCB, ZDT, EMC, TUC, Delta, Lite-On,
Kaori, ASE, KYEC, All Ring
Risk. Upside looks thin in the short term. Good earnings are not enough - look at
Nanya Tech and Memory sector today. Chasing momentum now may get hurt. I also
smelled some short unwind today (i.e. funding short names outperformance such
as Realtek, Novatek, Shipper, etc) - signs of de-leveraging. Directionless is how I
would describe the past few days, but maybe it's time to find some value Buy?

Max loss amount invested is 100%

value. There is always a fundamental risk of declining stock prices, which can cause losses to your investment. Some leveraged and
inverse ETFs and ETNs "reset" daily, meaning that they are designed to achieve their stated objectives on a daily basis. They will therefore
realize each day a loss or a gain which is a multiple (for leveraged ETFs and ETNs) or the inverse (for inverse ETFs and ETNs) of the

performance on that day of their underlying index or benchmark. For this reason, if they are held for a period longer than one day, their
performance over such longer periods of time can differ significantly from the stated multiple of the performance (or the inverse of the
performance) of their underlying index or benchmark during the same period of time. This effect can be magnified in volatile markets.
Prior to entering into a transaction in leveraged or inverse ETFs, you should be aware of the general risks associated with such

transactions. You should not enter into leveraged or inverse ETFs transactions unless you understand the nature and extent of your risk
exposure. You should also be satisfied that the leveraged or inverse ETFs transaction is appropriate for you in light of your circumstances
and financial condition. ETFs and ETNs that are linked to commodity futures do not offer direct exposure to the commodity's spot price,
and may perform differently than the spot price for the commodity itself. Performance differential can be magnified if a specific

condition persists in the market for a commodity that creates a disparity between near-term future prices and long-term future prices
and may lead to unexpected performance results. Other factors, such as roll yield, transaction costs, management fees, and taxes may
cause deviation in performance between the spot price of a commodity and commodity futures. You should not assume that an ETF or
ETN that is linked to commodity futures will provide an effective hedge because of a negative correlation with equities or other asset

swaps, and other derivatives as well as non-investment-grade securities - give rise to substantial risk and are not available to nor suitable
for all investors. If you have any questions about whether you are eligible to enter into these transactions with Goldman Sachs, please
contact your sales representative. Foreign-currency-denominated securities are subject to fluctuations in exchange rates that could have
an adverse effect on the value or price of, or income derived from, the investment. In addition, investors in securities such as ADRs, the

values of which are influenced by foreign currencies, effectively assume currency risk.

The scenario, forecasts and nowcasts of the Economic Research

### Page 2

UNITED STATES 
The US economy is expected to grow above its potential in 2026, with an average annual growth rate of 2. 2%, close to the 2025 rate (2.1%) . 
This apparent resilience to the uncertainty, tariff and energy shocks, together with above-trend productivity growth, masks K-shaped growth, 
driven by investment linked to AI-optimism and consumption by the wealthiest amid a wealth effect (historically high stock market valuations). 
Inflation overshooting is set to continue (3.4% in 2026) at least through 2028, largely because of the rise in oil prices and tariffs – although the 
impact of these latter appears to be less significant than expected. The labour market is showing clear signs of improvement that should bring 
the unemployment rate down toward 4.0%. Given this shift in price and employment risks amid dynamic growth, the FOMC proceeded with a 
first rate hike at September’s meeting and we expect two further 25bp increases in December 2026 and January 2027, bringing the Fed Funds 
target range at 4,25% - 4,50%.

CHINA 
Economic growth slowed to 4.3% y/y in Q2 2026 vs. 5.0% in Q1. It is projected to reach 4.6% in 2026 as a whole, down from 5% in 2025. Growth 
remains characterized by a K-shaped trajectory, and the gap between the strong performance of exports and the fragility of sectors that rely 
on domestic demand has widened this year. Domestic demand has weakened significantly in recent months. Retail sales have barely increased, 
the crisis in the property sector has continued and household confidence remains low. Fiscal policy measures aimed at supporting investment 
and private consumption will increase over the last months of the year . CPI inflation has rebounded slightly since last March, mostly due to 
the rise in global energy prices, as well as due to the increase in electronic good prices and anti-involution measures implemented by the 
authorities. Deflationary pressures persist, however, given weakness in domestic demand.

EUROZONE 
Eurozone growth is expected to slow to 1.1% in 2026 (from 1.3% in 2025), before strengthening to 1.5% in 2027, supported by higher defence 
and infrastructure spending as well as the global AI capex cycle. The economy has proven resilient to the energy shock, notab ly thanks to the 
increasingly tangible impact of G erman defence spending on the real economy. Elevated energy prices in the autumn (especially for natural 
gas), combined with resilient growth, are set to accelerate inflation to a peak around the turn of the year. Annual average i nflation is thus 
expected to reach  3.1% in 2026 (from 2.1% in 2025), before easing to 3.0% in 2027. Given persistent inflation and resilient growth, we 
anticipate another 25bp hike from the ECB in December 2026, bringing the deposit rate to 2.75%, followed by an extended pause . That said, 
stronger than expected second-round effects could lead to more tightening than our current baseline.

Contacts

tephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

### Page 3

FRANCE 
GDP growth remained low in Q2 (0% after -0.2% in Q1) as a result of deteriorated agricultural and construction output, as well as higher 
inflation (2.5% in 2026 according to our forecast, after 0.9% in 2025) . In 2026, GDP growth should  reach 0.5% (after 0.9% in 2025), driven 
mainly by exports.

UNITED KINGDOM 
UK GDP growth would improve slightly to 1.3% in 2026 and remain at this level in 2027  (1.2% in 2025). The economy has so far weathered 
both the energy price shock and domestic political volatility. That said, growth is expected to decelerate in the second half  of the year, while 
inflation is set to remain stubbornly above target (averaging 3.2% in both 2026 and 2027), driven by higher energy prices and mounting second-
round effects. In response, we expect the BoE to deliver a single insurance hike in November 2026, bringing the Bank Rate to 4.00%. The BoE 
is then expected to resume easing in H2 2027, with two cuts taking the Bank Rate to 3.50% by end-year.

JAPAN 
Following 1.2% in 2025, Japan's GDP growth is expected to decelerate to 1.0% in 2026, before rebounding to 1.2% in 2027. Higher inflation 
and production costs associated with the energy shock are expected to weigh on the economy’s overall performance. On the other hand, the 
latter is supported by fiscal policy  and AI-related investment. Inflation has generally overshot the 2% y/y target since 2022. Accordingly, the 
Bank of Japan initiated a cautious process of “adjustment in the degree of monetary accommodation” in 2024, lifting the policy rate to 1.25% 
so far (previously negative) – the highest since 1995. We expect two more hikes by the end of Q1 2027, followed by a return to a more cautious 
pace until the terminal rate of +2.5% is reached in H2 2028. Japan is facing long-term rates pressure, illustrated by historically high 10- and 30-
year yields, probably fueled by the level of public debt and the pace of monetary adjustment.

EXCHANGE RATES 
We expect Fed hikes to temporarily offset structural headwinds and support the dollar. EUR/USD would target 1.14 by end -2026, before the 
dollar's structural downtrend resumes toward 1.20 by end-2027, although political and fiscal risk may weigh on the euro next year. The USD/JPY 
appreciation would continue but at a more moderate pace, with the BoJ still behind the curve despite its hikes. By contrast, GBP/USD would 
see relative support underpinned by a modest fiscal risk premium under the new Labour government, and resilient growth.

高速 CCL 技術多路並進，HC 樹脂需求具上行空間 
What’s New – HC 樹脂具備較低 Dk/Df 的優勢 
從近年相關專利可以觀察到，碳氫樹脂在高頻高速 CCL 中的角色，已逐步由過去作為輔助型低
損耗樹脂，朝向可作為主要樹脂骨架的方向發展。部分專利中，MPPE 已不再是必要成分，配方
可直接以碳氫樹脂為主體，再搭配BMI、含磷樹脂、苯乙烯系共聚物或其他交聯材料，以補強耐
熱性、阻燃性、機械強度及銅箔黏著力。碳氫樹脂已具備由「配方中的其中一種樹脂」進一步成
為「主要低損耗樹脂平台」的可能性。惟目前專利仍主要代表技術可行性，尚不能直接推論高階
CCL 量產配方已全面轉向碳氫體系，後續仍須觀察其在黏銅、CTE、耐熱、阻燃、加工性及長期
可靠度等條件下的量產驗證結果。 
Trends/Outlook – CCL 材料升級方向仍處戰國時代 
展望下一階段，高速 CCL 的材料升級方向仍處於持續探索與驗證階段，目前尚未形成單一明確
的技術路線，目前市場上可觀察到的方向相當多元，包括採用更低損耗的玻纖布、降低銅箔表面
粗糙度、提高碳氫（HC）等低損耗樹脂的使用，以及進一步減少甚至移除玻纖布的無布（glass-
cloth-free）設計。因此，下一代高速材料究竟會延續「HC 樹脂＋低損耗玻纖布」架構，或進
一步發展至「HC 樹脂＋無機填料＋無布」 ，甚至採用 PTFE 無布等其他材料體系，目前仍有相
當大的不確定性，各種方案仍需在電性、可靠度、加工性與成本之間持續權衡。 
Investment Summary 
儘管下一代高速 CCL 的最終材料架構尚未定型，我們認為碳氫樹脂的重要性仍將顯著提升，但
不同技術路線對 HC 樹脂用量的帶動程度存在差異。若產業延續有布架構，隨著傳輸速率提升
及材料損耗要求趨嚴，樹脂本身的 Dk/Df 將持續成為決定 CCL 電性表現的關鍵因素，低損耗  
HC 樹脂的使用比例與性能要求均有望進一步提高；若材料進一步朝 HC 無布 CCL 或 HC 無布 
PP 發展，隨著玻纖布用量下降，原先由玻纖提供的部分尺寸穩定、CTE 控制及機械支撐功能，
將更多轉由 「HC 樹脂＋無機填料」 承擔，不僅可能提高單位面積介電材料中的 HC 樹脂用量，
也將使 HC 樹脂由單純提供低介電損耗，進一步成為決定整體材料熱性、機械性與加工性的核
心材料。

碳氫樹脂重要性逐漸提升 
HC 樹脂具備較低 Dk/Df 的優勢 
隨著 AI Server 持續升級，PCB 傳輸速率提升帶動低損耗材料需求增加，碳氫樹脂（Hydrocarbon 
Resin, HC）在高階 CCL 材料體系中的重要性逐步提升。相較 Epoxy、MPPE（Modified PPE ）、BMI
等其他樹脂體系，HC 樹脂具備較低 Dk/Df 的優勢，有助降低高速訊號傳輸損耗，因此已成為高
速低損耗 CCL 的重要樹脂體系之一。 
表一. 各高頻樹脂體系各有優劣

Epoxy 傳統 FR-4 體系 
1. 成本低 
2. 製程容忍度寬 
1. 較難滿足高頻高速需求

（Modified PPE）

再自行改質為 MPPE 
1. 低 Dk /低 Df，電性佳  1. 分子量較高，流動性差 
BMI 雙馬來醯亞胺 1. 高 Tg、高耐熱、高可靠度 1. 抗衝擊性較差，脆性大

實驗室與專利開發階段，碳氫樹脂已具備成為「主要低損耗樹脂平台」的可能性 
從近年相關專利可以觀察到，碳氫樹脂在高頻高速 CCL 中的角色，已逐步由過去作為輔助型低損
耗樹脂，朝向可作為主要樹脂骨架的方向發展。部分專利中，MPPE 已不再是必要成分，配方可
直接以碳氫樹脂為主體，再搭配 BMI、含磷樹脂、苯乙烯系共聚物或其他交聯材料，以補強耐熱
性、阻燃性、機械強度及銅箔黏著力。另有技術進一步透過接枝、官能化或分子結構設計，直接
改善碳氫樹脂原有的相容性與加工限制 。 這顯示在實驗室與專利開發階段 ， 碳氫樹脂已具備由 「配
方中的其中一種樹脂」進一步成為「主要低損耗樹脂平台」的可能性。惟目前專利仍主要代表技
術可行性 ， 尚不能直接推論高階CCL 量產配方已全面轉向碳氫體系，後續仍須觀察其在黏銅 、CTE、
耐熱、阻燃、加工性及長期可靠度等條件下的量產驗證結果。

3 
高速 CCL 技術多路並進，HC 樹脂需求具上行空間 
CCL 材料升級方向仍處戰國時代 
展望下一階段，高速 CCL 的材料升級方向仍處於持續探索與驗證階段，目前尚未形成單一明確
的技術路線，目前市場上可觀察到的方向相當多元，包括採用更低損耗的玻纖布、降低銅箔表面
粗糙度、提高碳氫（HC）等低損耗樹脂的使用，以及進一步減少甚至移除玻纖布的無布（glas s-
cloth-free）設計。因此，下一代高速材料究竟會延續「HC 樹脂＋低損耗玻纖布」架構，或進一
步發展至 「HC 樹脂＋無機填料＋無布」 ，甚至採用 PTFE 無布等其他材料體系，目前仍有相當大
的不確定性，各種方案仍需在電性、可靠度、加工性與成本之間持續權衡。 
HC 樹脂仍為高速材料關鍵 
儘管下一代高速 CCL 的最終材料架構尚未定型，我們認為碳氫樹脂的重要性仍將顯著提升，但
不同技術路線對 HC 樹脂用量的帶動程度存在差異。若產業延續有布架構，隨著傳輸速率提升及
材料損耗要求趨嚴，樹脂本身的 Dk/Df 將持續成為決定 CCL 電性表現的關鍵因素，低損耗 HC 
樹脂的使用比例與性能要求均有望進一步提高；若材料進一步朝 HC 無布 CCL 或 HC 無布 PP 
發展，隨著玻纖布用量下降，原先由玻纖提供的部分尺寸穩定、CTE 控制及機械支撐功能，將更
多轉由「HC 樹脂＋無機填料」承擔，不僅可能提高單位面積介電材料中的 HC 樹脂用量，也將
使 HC 樹脂由單純提供低介電損耗 ， 進一步成為決定整體材料熱性 、 機械性與加工性的核心材料 。 
另一方面，若部分最高速、最低損耗應用最終採用 PTFE 無布 CCL，其材料結構將主要由「PTFE
＋無機填料」構成，因此相較 HC 無布 CCL，PTFE 無布本身對 HC 樹脂的直接用量帶動相對有
限，甚至可能與 HC 無布 CCL 形成一定程度的技術路線競爭。然而，PTFE 無布若率先導入，也
不代表整體高速 PCB 將全面轉向 PTFE。考量不同板層與應用對傳輸損耗、加工性、可靠度及成
本的要求並不相同，未來高速 PCB 更可能呈現不同材料體系並存的情況，部分對損耗要求最嚴
格的層別或產品可能採用 PTFE 無布 ， 而其他大量高速 CCL、PP 或黏結層仍可能採用 HC 體系 。
特別是 PP 需要兼顧填孔、流動、壓合及層間接著等加工要求，因此即使部分 CCL 朝 PTFE 無布
發展，HC 樹脂仍可能透過 HC 有布 PP、HC 無布 PP，以及其他高速介電材料維持重要角色。

## Document 406: イスラエル総選挙の注目点 ～欧米との融和路線に変更なら、中東情勢に新たな展開も～

Source PDF: all report pdf/イスラエル総選挙の注目点 ～欧米との融和路線に変更なら、中東情勢に新たな展開も～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-09-29
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Global Trends

2026 年 9 月 29 日

1/4 
イスラエル総選挙の注目点 
～欧米との融和路線に変更なら、中東情勢に新たな展開も～ 
 経済調査部 シニア・フェロー  嶌峰 義清 
（℡：050-5474-7489）

（要旨） 
○ 10 月 27 日に実施予定のイスラエル総選挙は、2023 年 10 月のハマスによる攻撃を受けて以
降、初めての国政選挙となる。このため、現政権への批判も多く、与野党のどちらが勝利す
るかは蓋を開けるまで分からないとされている。 
○ 内政政策において与野党の主張に隔たりはある一方で、周辺地域を主とする安全保障政策に
ついては与野党間で大きな違いはない。しかし、欧米との関係悪化を厭わない政策を執る現
政権に対し、野党は周辺国も含めた外交にも配慮すべきとの姿勢を見せている。 
○ 野党が勝利した場合、特に欧州からの批判が強いヨルダン川西岸への対応が変化する可能性
がある。その先には2020年のアブラハム合意で関係が改善したUAEなどとの関係修復や、国
交正常化に向かっていたサウジアラビアとの交渉再開・進展も期待され、イスラエルが中東
外交における選択肢と影響力を回復することも考えられる。

1. 与野党拮抗するイスラエル総選挙 
10月 27日に実施が予定されているイスラエル総選挙は、2023年10 月7日のハマスによる大規
模なテロ攻撃を受けて以降、初めての国政選挙となる。同テロについては、攻撃を防ぐことがで
きなかった政府に対する批判が強く、野党は独立した調査機関による検証を訴えてきたものの、
ネタニヤフ首相はこれを拒否してきた。総選挙では、この問題が争点の一つになるとされてい
る。このほか、徴兵制度に関する問題（宗教学校（イェシバ）で学ぶハレディ（ユダヤ教超正統
派と呼ばれる人々）男性の多くが、兵役を事実上免除されている問題） 、政権による司法制度の権
限縮小への是非などが争点となっている。 
いずれの争点においても、今回の総選挙の最大の焦点は「ネタニヤフ政権を継続するか否か」
であり、政権存続の是非を問う選挙という位置づけとなっている。 
イスラエルの選挙は完全比例代表選挙で、全国単一の選挙区のもと120の議席を争う。こうし
た制度もあり、これまで単一の政党が単独で過半数の議席を得たことはなく、常に複数政党によ
る連立政権で国家運営されてきた。 
現在の勢力図は、ネタニヤフ首相のリクードが最大議席を有する与党が64議席、中道政党のイ

### Page 2

2/4 
ェシュ・アティドが最大議席を有する野党が56議席となっている（図表1）。与党は6政党による
連立で、極右や超正統派と言われる宗教保守派の政党がまとまった形となっている。一方の野党
は7 政党で、中道や左派だけではなく、右派系の党もあり、反ネタニヤフ政権という点が合致点
だ。 
ただし、今回の選挙の目玉候補は、イスラエル国防軍の元参謀総長で、前回選挙（2022年）で
は政党連合「国家団結」から出馬して当選、第6次ネタニヤフ政権の戦時内閣に入ったガディ・
エイゼンコット氏だ。同氏は2025年に中道政党ヤシャル（「真っ直ぐ」の意）を立ち上げた。国
民の人気も高く、各種世論調査では10月の選挙で最大与党リクードと並ぶ議席を確保するとの予
想が目立つ。現在は議会に議席を保有していないヤシャルだが、選挙では最大議席を獲得する可
能性もある。

2. 世界が注目するイスラエルの安全保障政策が大きく変わることはない 
イスラエル国内では、ネタニヤフ首相への信任と、徴兵制度など内政に関する問題が主な争点
となるが、海外から見た最大の注目点は、イスラエルのパレスチナへの対応に変化が出るかどう
か、といった点だろう。 
ただし、パレスチナ問題への短期的な安全保障対応では、世論調査などで最大議席を争う与党
リクードとヤシャルとの間に大きな差はない（図表2） 。ヨルダン川西岸のユダヤ人入植地につい
ては両党とも一定程度支持しているほか、ハマスに対しては軍事・統治能力の排除を重視してい
る。両党とも現状では政治的和平を先行させるよりも、ハマスの軍事・統治能力の排除やイスラ
エルの安全保障確保を優先する立場を取っている。 
国連加盟国のうち8 割超（161カ国／193カ国）がパレスチナを国家として承認しているが、両
党とも現状での早期のパレスチナ国家樹立には否定的である。一方、ネタニヤフ首相が将来的な
パレスチナ国家の樹立にも否定的な立場を明確にしているのに対し、ヤシャルは将来的な政治的

分離の選択肢を完全に閉ざすような政策には慎重である。ヨルダン川西岸やガザ地区への対応に
ついては、両党ともイスラエルの安全保障確保のための軍事行動を重視しているものの、リクー

保障統制を主張しているのに対し、ヤシャルは西岸の事実上の併合や、将来的な政治的分離を困

には距離を置く一方、安全保障上の軍事的自由は重視している。

3. 野党勝利で変化する可能性のあるイスラエル外交 
ヤシャルなどの野党勢力が選挙で勝利し、政権交代が実現した場合、イスラエルの外交政策に
は一定の変化が生じる可能性がある。前述の通り、ヤシャルを率いるアイゼンコット氏も、ハマ
スの軍事・統治能力の排除やイスラエルの安全保障確保を重視し、現状でのパレスチナ国家樹立
にも否定的である。このため、政権交代によってイスラエルの安全保障政策が直ちに融和路線へ
転換するとは考えにくい。 
一方で、外交の「手法」にはリクードとの違いがみられる。アイゼンコット氏は、欧州諸国と
の関係や国際的な正統性もイスラエルの安全保障を支える重要な要素と捉えており、英国やフラ
ンスをはじめとする伝統的な友好国との関係修復を重視している。実際、英国やフランスは2024
年のイランによるイスラエル攻撃の際に迎撃に協力しており、アイゼンコット氏はこうした諸国
との安全保障協力を重視する姿勢を示している。また、ヤシャルは党の目標として、軍事・経済
面での強さを背景に、地域各国との和平協定や協力関係を追求することを掲げている。 
このため野党政権が成立すれば、イスラエルの安全保障上の基本方針を維持しながら、ネタニ
ヤフ政権下で悪化した欧米諸国との関係や国際的な評価の修復を図る方向に外交姿勢が変化する
可能性がある。もっとも、アイゼンコット氏は欧米諸国が支持する二国家解決を現時点では否定
しており、欧米の要求に合わせてパレスチナ政策を大幅に転換することを意味するわけではな
い。むしろ、安全保障上の原則を維持しつつ、欧米との不必要な対立を抑制する姿勢に近いと考
論点 リクード ヤシャル

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
4/4 
えられる。 
こうした変化は、中東諸国との関係にも影響する可能性がある。サウジアラビアはイスラエル
との国交正常化について、パレスチナ国家樹立に向けた信頼できる道筋を事実上の条件としてお
り、現状では正常化に向けたハードルは依然高い。加えて、2026年のイランを巡る軍事衝突を経
て、サウジをはじめとする湾岸諸国はイランとの全面的な対立を回避する姿勢も強めており、イ
スラエルの政権交代だけで直ちに正常化が実現するとは考えにくい。アイゼンコット氏自身も、
サウジとの正常化は当面困難との認識を示している。 
それでも、西岸の事実上の併合や過度な入植拡大、入植者による暴力などを抑制し、ガザを巡
る政治プロセスへの参加姿勢を強めれば、アラブ諸国がイスラエルとの外交関係を改善する余地
は現在より広がり得る。実際、ヤシャルはイスラエルの軍事的な行動の自由を維持しながらも、
米国、欧州、アラブ諸国との関係を再構築し、軍事力によって得た成果を外交交渉に活用するこ
とをより重視しているとみられる。 
その場合、中長期的にはイスラエルとサウジアラビアやUAE、エジプト、ヨルダンなどとの政
治・安全保障協力が再び進展する可能性があり、イランを巡る地域の勢力関係にも影響を及ぼし
得る。ただし、湾岸諸国はイスラエルとの関係強化と同時にイランとの関係安定化も重視してい
る。このため、政権交代後に想定されるのは、単純な「イスラエル・アラブ諸国対イラン」とい
う対立構造の復活というよりも、イスラエルが欧米・アラブ諸国との関係を修復することで、中
東外交における選択肢と影響力を回復していく展開と考える方が適切だろう。

以 上

## Document 437: ロシア、財政運営はウクライナ戦争のさらなる長期化を鮮明に ～2027年度予算案はウクライナ戦争の長期化を前提、対日関係改善の道_bc450c02

Source PDF: all report pdf/ロシア、財政運営はウクライナ戦争のさらなる長期化を鮮明に ～2027年度予算案はウクライナ戦争の長期化を前提、対日関係改善の道_bc450c02.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

World Trends

2026 年 10 月 2 日

1/3 
ロシア、財政運営はウクライナ戦争のさらなる長期化を鮮明に 
～2027 年度予算案はウクライナ戦争の長期化を前提、対日関係改善の道筋も見えにくい～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ ロシア政府が提出した 2027 年度予算案では、歳出 48.8 兆ルーブルのうち国防費が 17.1 兆
ルーブルと全体の35％を占め、 今年度予算から４割以上増加するとしている。 安全保障機関
向けを含めれば関連支出は歳出全体の４割を上回り、プーチン政権がウクライナ戦争のさら
なる長期化を財政面で織り込んでいることがうかがえる。 
○ 一方、 戦争の長期化と国防費の膨張により財政状態は悪化している。2027 年の歳入は43.3 兆
ルーブルを見込み、原油・ガス収入の伸び悩みを、付加価値税や法人税などの税収増や追加
増税で補う方針である。財政赤字はＧＤＰ比▲2.2％と今年度の▲3.2％から縮小する想定と
しているが、歳出・債務を機動的に増やせる制度が整えられているため、さらなる財政悪化
の可能性は残る。公的債務残高も 2027 年末時点でＧＤＰ比 21.7％と、政府が従来「安全ラ
イン」としてきた20％を上回る見通しであり、戦費確保と財政健全性の両立が課題となる。 
○ こうしたなか、プーチン大統領は日本との協力に関心を示す一方、日本の「軍事化」やアジ
ア太平洋地域での安全保障枠組みに懸念を表明し、政治・安全保障面で日本をけん制した。
また、 ウクライナ戦争終結後の日ロ関係改善にも言及したものの、2027 年度予算案は戦争の
早期終結を前提としておらず、短期的には困難な状況にある。日ロ間では政治・安全保障上
の対立が続くため、経済関係だけを切り離して改善することは容易ではなく、日本にはロシ
ア側の具体的な政策・行動を見極めながら慎重に対応することが求められる。

【2027 年度予算で国防費は前年比４割超増、戦争継続と財政健全化の両立に難題】 
ロシア政府は９月 30 日、2027 年度予算案を連邦議会下院（国家院）に提出した。ウクライナ戦
争の開戦から４年半以上が経過するなか、ロシア経済にとっては軍事関連が一大産業となっている。
そのため、予算案における軍事費の動向は、ウクライナ戦争の先行きに加え、ロシア経済の行方を
大きく左右することが予想される。2027 年の歳出規模は 48.8 兆ルーブルとなり、そのうち国防費
は 17.1 兆ルーブルと歳出全体の35％を占める。国防費の規模は今年度予算（12.1 兆ルーブル）か
ら４割以上増加、従来計画（13.5 兆ルーブル）と比較しても３割弱膨らんだ。なお、国防費とは別
に連邦政府の安全保障機関（対外情報庁（ＳＶＲ）や連邦保安庁（ＦＳＢ）など）に関連予算が計

### Page 2

2/3 
上されており、 これらを合わせると歳出全体の４割以上にのぼる。 こうした予算配分を勘案すると、
プーチン政権がウクライナ戦争のさらなる長期化を前提としている様子がうかがえる。 
ウクライナ戦争の長期化やそれに伴う国防費の増大を受けて、財政状況は急速に悪化している。
予算案では、2027 年の歳入規模を 43.3 兆ルーブル、そのうち原油・ガス関連収入は７兆ルーブル
とした。前提となるウラル原油価格は１バレル＝53 ドルとしている。 足元の原油価格は高止まりし
ているものの、 今年度の原油・ガス関連収入を8.9 兆ルーブルから7.6 兆ルーブルに下方修正して
いる。背景には、原油価格やウラル原油のディスカウント、原油・ガスの生産・輸出動向など複数
の要因があるとみられる。一方で、足元ではインフレが加速するなか、付加価値税（ＶＡＴ）や物
品税、 輸入関税などの税収は今年度予算から１割程度の増加を見込むほか、 法人税も同様に増加す
るとの見方を示す。さらに、膨張する国防費を賄うべく、金融所得などを対象とする追加増税も検
討しており、財政悪化に歯止めをかけたいとの狙いもうかがえる。 
その結果、2027 年の財政赤字は 5.5 兆ルーブル、ＧＤＰ比で▲2.2％となる。足元の財政状況が
悪化していることを受けて、今年度の財政赤字はＧＤＰ比▲3.2％と当初案（同▲1.6％）から赤字
幅が大幅に引き上げられたものの、一転して来年度の赤字幅は縮小するとしている。ただし、今年
６月に連邦議会は予算法の改正を経ない形で歳出、債務の拡大を可能にする法律を可決しており、
国防費をはじめとする歳出を機動的に増加させることが可能となっている。政府によれば、2026 年
だけを対象とする特例措置としたものの、 先日実施された総選挙では与党が３分の２を超える議席
を獲得しており （注１） 、 先行きもなし崩し的に同様の対応が進む可能性には注意が必要である。公
的債務残高は 2027 年末時点でＧＤＰ比21.7％と国際的には低水準にとどまるものの、政府がこれ
まで「安全ライン」 とみなしてきた20％と上回る。 このため、 戦争継続に伴う歳出圧力と財政健全
性の維持をいかに両立させるか、ロシア政府にとって一段と重要な課題となろう。

【安全保障面で日本をけん制する一方、経済協力の余地は残したいロシア】 
プーチン大統領は１日、国内外の有識者が集まる国際会議（ワルダイ会議）に登壇し、日本との
関係について、協力に関心があるとする一方、日本の「軍事化」の動きに懸念を抱いていると述べ
たと報じられている。プーチン氏はこのところ、中国が国際場裡で日本を批判する際に用いる「新
型軍国主義」とのナラティブ（物語）に歩調をあわせる動きをみせており、今回の発言もそうした
文脈の延長線上にあると考えられる。また、アジア太平洋地域の安全保障環境を巡って、軍事・政
治的なブロック形成に懸念を抱いていると述べるなど、日米豪印戦略対話（Quad）や米英豪による
安全保障枠組み （AUKUS） を念頭に置いた可能性がある。 一方で、 ウクライナ戦争の終了後には日ロ
関係が改善するとの見通しを示し、 日本側に関係改善に向けた対応を求めた。 しかし、2027 年度予
算案の内容は、 早期にウクライナ戦争が終結する可能性が低いことを示唆しており、 現実的ではな
いと捉える必要があろう。プーチン氏の発言は、政治・安全保障面で日本をけん制する一方、経済

议案计划，让美财政部此前发布的涉及俄罗斯柴油销售、进⼝等多项俄能源相关⼀般许可

摘要：
美国总统特朗普放宽俄罗斯柴油出⼝限制的决定遭到国会两党议员集体阻击。
美东时间 16 ⽇周五，美国众议院外交事务委员会的⺠主党⾸席委员 Gregory Meeks 提出⼀项两
